  5353: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_static_assert_evaluate(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CParseResult* result,
  5354:                                                         CDeclaration declaration, CScopeId scope, u64* value_out, String8* message_out,
  5355:                                                         bool* requires_typed_evaluation_out)
  5356: {
  5357:     if (requires_typed_evaluation_out)
  5358:     {
  5359:         *requires_typed_evaluation_out = false;
  5360:     }
  5361:     u32 start = declaration.token_start;
  5362:     u32 end = start + declaration.token_count;
  5363:     bool valid = start + 3 < end && end <= preprocess.token_count &&
  5364:                  c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[start + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS);
  5365:     u32 comma = end;
  5366:     u32 close = end;
  5367:     u32 depth = 0;
  5368:     // Braces and brackets carry commas of their own -- a compound literal
  5369:     // operand (`_Static_assert(sizeof (struct S){1, 2} == 8, "...")`) or a
  5370:     // subscript -- and neither can close the assert's parenthesis, so they get
  5371:     // their own depth. Counting only parentheses takes the literal's first
  5372:     // comma for the message separator and truncates the expression.
  5373:     u32 group_depth = 0;
  5374:     for (u32 token_index = start + 1; valid && token_index < end; token_index += 1)
  5375:     {
  5376:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[token_index];
  5377:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET))
  5378:         {
  5379:             group_depth += 1;
  5380:         }
  5381:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACKET))
  5382:         {
  5383:             group_depth -= group_depth != 0;
  5384:         }
  5385:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
  5386:         {
  5387:             depth += 1;
  5388:         }
  5389:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS))
  5390:         {
  5391:             if (!depth)
  5392:             {
  5393:                 valid = false;
  5394:             }
  5395:             else if (!--depth)
  5396:             {
  5397:                 close = token_index;
  5398:                 break;
  5399:             }
  5400:         }
  5401:         else if (depth == 1 && !group_depth && comma == end && c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))
  5402:         {
  5403:             comma = token_index;
  5404:         }
  5405:     }
  5406:     u32 expression_end = comma < close ? comma : close;
  5407:     valid = valid && close != end && expression_end > start + 2;
  5408:     if (valid)
  5409:     {
  5410:         if (comma < close && comma + 1 < close && preprocess.tokens[comma + 1].kind == C_TOKEN_STRING_LITERAL)
  5411:         {
  5412:             *message_out = c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[comma + 1]);
  5413:         }
  5414:         valid = c_parse_constant_expression_evaluate(machine, arena, preprocess, result, scope, start + 2, expression_end,
  5415:                                                       value_out, requires_typed_evaluation_out);
  5416:     }
  5417:     return valid;
  5418: }

  5547: // The tokens [start, end) as a diagnostic quotes them: on one line, touching
  5548: // where they touched in the source and otherwise one space apart, so a line
  5549: // break, a comment or a macro boundary reads as the space it stood for. This
  5550: // is the -E printer's adjacency rule (compiler_driver_preprocess_text) with
  5551: // line breaks folded, guarded by the same c_token_requires_separator.
  5552: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL String8 c_parse_token_range_text(Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 start, u32 end)
  5553: {
  5554:     u64 capacity = 1;
  5555:     for (u32 index = start; index < end; index += 1)
  5556:     {
  5557:         capacity += c_token_length(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[index]) + 1;
  5558:     }
  5559:     char8* text = arena_allocate(arena, char8, capacity);
  5560:     u64 length = 0;
  5561:     CSourceLocation previous_location = {0};
  5562:     CToken previous_token = {0};
  5563:     String8 previous_spelling = {0};
  5564:     for (u32 index = start; index < end; index += 1)
  5565:     {
  5566:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
  5567:         CSourceLocation location = c_preprocess_token_location(&preprocess, token);
  5568:         String8 spelling = c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, token);
  5569:         if (index != start &&
  5570:             (location.file != previous_location.file || location.line != previous_location.line ||
  5571:              location.column != previous_location.column + previous_spelling.length ||
  5572:              c_token_requires_separator(previous_token, previous_spelling, token, spelling)))
  5573:         {
  5574:             text[length++] = ' ';
  5575:         }
  5576:         memcpy(text + length, spelling.pointer, spelling.length);
  5577:         length += spelling.length;
  5578:         previous_location = location;
  5579:         previous_token = token;
  5580:         previous_spelling = spelling;
  5581:     }
  5582:     text[length] = 0;
  5583:     return (String8){
  5584:         .pointer = text,
  5585:         .length = length,
  5586:     };
  5587: }
  5588: 
  5589: // Whether a type name that ends at `cursor`, short of its operand's `)`, is
  5590: // malformed there. An opening parenthesis may begin an abstract function
  5591: // declarator (`int (*)(void)`) and a reserved or qualifier word may still be
  5592: // part of the type, so only other tokens prove the `)` missing.

  5597:     {

  5599:     }

 22804:         // A member assertion is deferred from its aggregate's scope; the
 22805:         // innermost block enclosing it is where its names bind.
 22806:         assertion.scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, assertion.scope, assertion.token_start);
 22807:         CDeclaration declaration = {.token_start = assertion.token_start, .token_count = assertion.token_count};
 22808:         u32 start = 0;
 22809:         u32 end = 0;
 22810:         u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22811:         CParseConstant value = {.type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID};
 22812:         if (c_parse_static_assert_expression_range(preprocess, declaration, &start, &end))
 22813:         {
 22814:             value = c_parse_typed_constant(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, assertion.scope, start, end);
 22815:         }
 22816:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 22817:         if (!value.valid)
 22818:         {
 22819:             c_parse_diagnostic(result, assertion.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_STATIC_ASSERT_NOT_CONSTANT,
 22820:                                S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression"));
 22821:         }
 22822:         else if (value.is_float || !c_parse_constant_truth(value))
 22823:         {
 22824:             c_parse_diagnostic(result, assertion.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_STATIC_ASSERT_FAILED,
 22825:                                S8("static assertion expression is not a true integer constant expression"));
 22826:         }
 22827:     }
 22828:     BUSTER_UNUSED(arena);

  5892: {
  5893:     if (!arena || !alignment || !arena->granularity || alignment - 1 > UINT64_MAX - arena->position)
  5894:     {
  5895:         return false;
  5896:     }
  5897:     u64 position = (arena->position + alignment - 1) & ~(alignment - 1);
  5898:     if (position > arena->reserved_size || size > arena->reserved_size - position)
  5899:     {
  5900:         return false;
  5901:     }

  5904:     if (granularity - 1 > UINT64_MAX - end)
  5905:     {
  5906:         return false;
  5907:     }

  1608: // A bit-field width written as one literal token that is not all decimal
  1609: // digits, or a bit-field whose declarator is parenthesized, used to fail the
  1610: // member segment and drop the whole aggregate. The static assertions check the
  1611: // parse-time layout and the program checks the lowered one; the values match
  1612: // clang 18.
  1613: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_bit_field_width_spellings(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
  1614: {
  1615:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
  1616:     String8 source = S8("struct spelled_bits { int a : 0x3; unsigned b : 3u; int c : 0b11; unsigned d : 1'0;\n"
  1617:                         "    int (x) : 3U; unsigned (y) : 0x20; unsigned w : 010; };\n"

  1621:                         "int main(void) {\n"
  1622:                         "    object.a = -2; object.b = 7; object.c = 3; object.d = 1023;\n"
  1623:                         "    object.x = -4; object.y = 0xffffffffu; object.w = 0x3ff;\n"
  1624:                         "    if (object.a != -2 || object.b != 7 || object.c != 3 || object.d != 1023) return 1;\n"
  1625:                         "    if (object.x != -4 || object.y != 0xffffffffu || object.w != 255) return 2;\n"
  1626:                         "    return 0;\n"
  1627:                         "}\n");

  4859: // A member declaration naming only a tag, or a typedef for an untagged
  4860: // aggregate, declares nothing; only an untagged definition written in place
  4861: // is an anonymous member (C11 6.7.2.1p13). The layouts are GCC's and Clang's
  4862: // without -fms-extensions on x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu (#1706).
  4863: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_tagged_member_declares_nothing(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
  4864: {
  4865:     UnitTestResult result = {0};

  4871:     target.os = OPERATING_SYSTEM_LINUX;
  4872:     CIRLowerResult lowered = c_test_lower_source(temporary.arena,
  4873:         S8("struct S { int a; }; union U { int a; double d; }; typedef struct { int z; } A;\n"
  4874:            "struct T { struct S; int b; };\n"
  4875:            "struct V { union U; int b; };\n"
  4876:            "struct W { A; int b; };\n"
  4877:            "struct Y { struct N { int n; }; int b; };\n"
  4878:            "struct Z { struct Fwd; int b; };\n"
  4879:            "struct X { struct { int p; }; const union { int q; char r[8]; }; int b; };\n"
  4880:            "_Static_assert(sizeof(struct T) == 4 && __builtin_offsetof(struct T, b) == 0, \"tagged struct\");\n"
  4881:            "_Static_assert(sizeof(struct V) == 4 && __builtin_offsetof(struct V, b) == 0, \"tagged union\");\n"
  4882:            "_Static_assert(sizeof(struct W) == 4 && __builtin_offsetof(struct W, b) == 0, \"typedef name\");\n"

  4884:            "_Static_assert(sizeof(struct Z) == 4, \"forward tag\");\n"
  4885:            "_Static_assert(sizeof(struct X) == 16 && __builtin_offsetof(struct X, b) == 12, \"anonymous members\");\n"

  5108: // A non-constant static assertion quotes its whole controlling expression.
  5109: // The quote once stopped at the first `,` or `)` at any depth (#1573), so a
  5110: // parenthesized operand or a call printed truncated and unbalanced. Tokens
  5111: // keep their source adjacency; a comment, line break or macro boundary reads
  5112: // as one space. A `_Generic` assertion is deferred and its diagnostic, which
  5113: // quotes nothing, is pinned unchanged.
  5114: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_static_assert_nonconstant_quote(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
  5115: {
  5116:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
  5117:     struct
  5118:     {
  5119:         String8 source;
  5120:         String8 message;
  5121:     } cases[] = {
  5122:         {S8("int value;\n_Static_assert(value, \"simple\");\n"),
  5123:          S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression: value")},
  5124:         {S8("int value;\n_Static_assert((value + 1) != 0, \"parenthesized\");\n"),
  5125:          S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression: (value + 1) != 0")},
  5126:         {S8("int pair(int, int);\n_Static_assert(pair(1, 2), \"call\");\n"),
  5127:          S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression: pair(1, 2)")},
  5128:         {S8("int pair(int, int);\n_Static_assert((1 << 32) != 0 && pair(1, 2), \"both\");\n"),
  5129:          S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression: (1 << 32) != 0 && pair(1, 2)")},
  5130:         {S8("int pair(int, int);\n_Static_assert(pair(1, 2));\n"),
  5131:          S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression: pair(1, 2)")},
  5132:         {S8("int local(int n) { _Static_assert((n + 1) * 2, \"local\"); return n; }\n"),
  5133:          S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression: (n + 1) * 2")},
  5134:         {S8("int pair(int, int);\n_Static_assert(pair(/* first */ 1,\n    2) + pair( 3 , 4 ), \"layout\");\n"),
  5135:          S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression: pair( 1, 2) + pair( 3 , 4 )")},
  5136:         {S8("#define NEGATIVE -1\nint pair(int, int);\n_Static_assert(pair(-NEGATIVE, 2), \"macro\");\n"),
  5137:          S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression: pair(- - 1 , 2)")},
  5138:         {S8("int pair(int, int);\n_Static_assert(_Generic(0, int: pair(1, 2), default: 0), \"generic\");\n"),
  5139:          S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression")},
  5140:     };
  5141:     for (u32 case_index = 0; case_index < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(cases); case_index += 1)
  5142:     {
  5143:         TemporalArena temporary = scratch_begin(0, 0);
  5144:         CPreprocessResult tokens = c_preprocess(temporary.arena, cases[case_index].source,
  5145:                                                 (CPreprocessOptions){
  5146:                                                     .target = target_native,
  5147:                                                     .data_layout = target_data_layout(target_native),
  5148:                                                 });
  5149:         CParserResult syntax = c_parse_ast(temporary.arena, tokens);
  5150:         CIRLowerResult analysis = c_analyze(temporary.arena, S8("static-assert-quote.c"), tokens, syntax, target_native);
  5151:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, tokens.diagnostic_count == 0);
  5152:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, syntax.diagnostic_count == 0);
  5153:         BUSTER_TEST_RAW(arguments, analysis.diagnostic_count == 1, cases[case_index].source);
  5154:         if (analysis.diagnostic_count == 1)
  5155:         {
  5156:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, analysis.diagnostics[0].kind == C_DIAGNOSTIC_STATIC_ASSERT_NOT_CONSTANT);
  5157:             BUSTER_STRING_TEST(arguments, analysis.diagnostics[0].message, cases[case_index].message);
  5158:         }
  5159:         scratch_end(temporary);
  5160:     }
  5161:     return result;
  5162: }

  9301:     // #640: __is_target_os answers for the selected compilation target. The
  9302:     // conditions below use nothing but the target queries, so a wrong answer
  9303:     // reaches #error instead of being masked by a target macro that happens to
  9304:     // be defined; the probe declaration that follows proves the guarded region
  9305:     // was actually preprocessed rather than skipped. Spellings and aliases are
  9306:     // clang 18's, read back with -target and -E: Windows answers `win32` too,
  9307:     // a Darwin target answers `darwin` beside its own name, and an Android
  9308:     // target answers `linux` because its triple's OS is Linux and `android` is
  9309:     // the environment, never an OS spelling.
  9310:     typedef struct CTargetOsQueryCase CTargetOsQueryCase;
  9311:     struct CTargetOsQueryCase
  9312:     {
  9313:         Target target;
  9314:         String8 source;
  9315:         String8 probe;
  9316:     };
  9317:     CTargetOsQueryCase target_os_cases[] = {
  9318:         {
  9319:             .target = {.cpu_arch = CPU_ARCH_X86_64, .os = OPERATING_SYSTEM_WINDOWS},
  9320:             .source = S8("#if !__is_target_os(windows) || !__is_target_os(win32)\n"
  9321:                          "#error windows target OS\n"
  9322:                          "#endif\n"
  9323:                          "#if __is_target_os(linux) || __is_target_os(macos) || __is_target_os(macosx) || "
  9324:                          "__is_target_os(ios) || __is_target_os(darwin) || __is_target_os(uefi) || __is_target_os(android)\n"
  9325:                          "#error windows target cross-OS\n"
  9326:                          "#endif\n"
  9327:                          "#if !__is_target_arch(x86_64) || __is_target_vendor(apple)\n"
  9328:                          "#error windows target arch or vendor\n"
  9329:                          "#endif\n"
  9330:                          "int windows_os_probe;\n"),
  9331:             .probe = S8("windows_os_probe"),
  9332:         },
  9333:         {
  9334:             .target = {.cpu_arch = CPU_ARCH_AARCH64, .os = OPERATING_SYSTEM_ANDROID, .os_version_major = 35},
  9335:             .source = S8("#if !__is_target_os(linux)\n"
  9336:                          "#error android target OS\n"
  9337:                          "#endif\n"
  9338:                          "#if __is_target_os(android) || __is_target_os(windows) || __is_target_os(win32) || "

  9340:                          "#error android target cross-OS\n"
  9341:                          "#endif\n"

 12745: // #629: the integer-range evaluator must use the source token indices. With
 12746: // compact lexing, copying the expression under an assertion wrapper borrowed
 12747: // the original shape sidecar and rejected this valid sizeof compound literal.
 12748: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_token_view_constant_range(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
 12749: {
 12750:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
 12751:     for (u32 form = 0; form < 2; form += 1)
 12752:     {
 12753:         TemporalArena temporary = scratch_begin(0, 0);
 12754:         CPreprocessResult preprocess = c_preprocess(temporary.arena,
 12755:             S8("struct TokenViewPair { int x, y; };\n"
 12756:                "#pragma pack(push, 1)\n"
 12757:                "struct TokenViewPacked { char c; int x; };\n"
 12758:                "#pragma pack(pop)\n"
 12759:                "int unrelated = 7;\n"
 12760:                "constexpr int size = sizeof (struct TokenViewPair){1, 2};\n"
 12761:                "constexpr int packed_size = sizeof (struct TokenViewPacked){1, 2};\n"
 12762:                "_Static_assert(sizeof (struct TokenViewPacked){1, 2} == 5, \"compound literal, comma\");\n"
 12763:                "int array[(size == 8 && packed_size == 5) ? 1 : -1];\n"),
 12764:             (CPreprocessOptions){.target = target_native, .data_layout = target_data_layout(target_native),
 12765:                                  .dialect = C_PREPROCESS_DIALECT_C23});
 12766:         CParseResult parse = c_parse(temporary.arena, preprocess);
 12767:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, preprocess.diagnostic_count == 0);
 12768:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, parse.diagnostic_count == 0);
 12769:         if (BUSTER_REQUIRE(arguments, preprocess.diagnostic_count == 0 && parse.diagnostic_count == 0))
 12770:         {
 12771:             bool found_size = false;
 12772:             bool found_packed_size = false;
 12773:             for (u32 entity_index = 0; entity_index < parse.entity_count; entity_index += 1)
 12774:             {
 12775:                 CEntity* entity = parse.entities + entity_index;
 12776:                 found_size |= string_equal(entity->name, S8("size")) && entity->has_constant_value && entity->constant_value == 8;
 12777:                 found_packed_size |= string_equal(entity->name, S8("packed_size")) && entity->has_constant_value && entity->constant_value == 5;
 12778:             }
 12779:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, found_size);
 12780:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, found_packed_size);

 12787:             }
 12788:         }
 12789:         scratch_end(temporary);
 12790:     }
 12791:     return result;
 12792: }

 12794: // A type name may spell a function -- `int(void)` -- not just a pointer to one
 12795: // -- `int (*)(void)`. The type-name resolution accepted only the pointer
 12796: // declarator, so a bare function declarator left the whole type name unresolved
 12797: // and sizeof over it fell back to the prediction's int guess, folding 4 where
 12798: // GNU folds 1 (tests/basic_c_sizeof_function_designator.c is the runtime
 12799: // fixture). The array bounds below are where a misfolded size shows in the IR.
 12800: // The variadic spelling `int(int, ...)` is one of them: the ellipsis is a
 12801: // parameter of the type name, not of the declarator the bound belongs to, so
 12802: // spelled_variadic below must measure like every other function type.
 12803: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_sizeof_function_type_name(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
 12804: {
 12805:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
 12806:     BUSTER_UNUSED(arguments);

 12911: // `void` is one byte in both layout engines, and an object of it is still
 12912: // refused. GNU gives `void` a size so that a `void *` steps by bytes, and both
 12913: // reference compilers fold `sizeof(void)`, `sizeof(const void)` and
 12914: // `_Alignof(void)` to 1; this compiler folded 0, so `p + 3` scaled the index
 12915: // by nothing and answered the base address with no diagnostic (#743). The
 12916: // size and completeness are different questions, though, and the refusals used
 12917: // to fall out of the size: an unresolved aggregate layout, a zero alignment,
 12918: // or -- for a file-scope object -- a code-generation failure that named `main`
 12919: // and never named the object. Each of them is asked of the kind now, through
 12920: // c_ir_type_is_void_object, so this test is both halves at once. The runtime
 12921: // answers are tests/basic_c_void_size.c, under all four register allocators.
 12922: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_void_object_refusals(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
 12923: {
 12924:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
 12925:     BUSTER_UNUSED(arguments);
 12926:     // The parse engine folds the static assertions and the array bounds; the
 12927:     // IR engine builds the type the pointer below points at. Both have to
 12928:     // answer 1, which is the rule two layout engines share.
 12929:     TemporalArena size_temporary = scratch_begin(0, 0);
 12930:     CPreprocessResult size_tokens = c_preprocess(size_temporary.arena,
 12931:                                                  S8("typedef void VoidAlias;\n"
 12932:                                                     "_Static_assert(sizeof(void) == 1, \"sizeof void\");\n"
 12933:                                                     "_Static_assert(sizeof(const void) == 1, \"sizeof const void\");\n"
 12934:                                                     "_Static_assert(sizeof(volatile void) == 1, \"sizeof volatile void\");\n"
 12935:                                                     "_Static_assert(sizeof(VoidAlias) == 1, \"sizeof void alias\");\n"
 12936:                                                     "_Static_assert(_Alignof(void) == 1, \"alignof void\");\n"
 12937:                                                     "static unsigned char folded_bound[sizeof(void) + 6];\n"
 12938:                                                     "void *void_pointer;\n"
 12939:                                                     "unsigned long void_step(void *p, void *q)\n"
 12940:                                                     "{ return (unsigned long)((unsigned char *)(p + 3) - (unsigned char *)p) + (unsigned long)(q - p); }\n"),
 12941:                                                  (CPreprocessOptions){0});
 12942:     CParseResult size_parse = c_parse(size_temporary.arena, size_tokens);
 12943:     CIRLowerResult size_ir = c_lower_to_ir(size_temporary.arena, S8("void-size.c"), size_tokens, size_parse, target_native);
 12944:     BUSTER_TEST(arguments, size_tokens.diagnostic_count == 0);
 12945:     BUSTER_TEST(arguments, size_parse.diagnostic_count == 0);
 12946:     BUSTER_TEST(arguments, size_ir.diagnostic_count == 0);
 12947:     BUSTER_TEST(arguments, size_ir.program != 0);
 12948:     if (size_ir.program)
 12949:     {
 12950:         IrModule* module = &size_ir.program->modules[0];
 12951:         IrType* folded_bound_type = 0;
 12952:         IrType* void_pointer_type = 0;
 12953:         for (u32 global_index = 0; global_index < module->global_count; global_index += 1)
 12954:         {
 12955:             IrGlobal* global = module->globals + global_index;
 12956:             IrSymbol* symbol = ir_symbol_from_id(&size_ir.program->symbols, global->symbol);
 12957:             if (symbol && string_equal(symbol->link_name, S8("folded_bound")))
 12958:             {
 12959:                 folded_bound_type = ir_type_from_id(&size_ir.program->types, global->type);
 12960:             }
 12961:             if (symbol && string_equal(symbol->link_name, S8("void_pointer")))
 12962:             {
 12963:                 void_pointer_type = ir_type_from_id(&size_ir.program->types, global->type);
 12964:             }
 12965:         }
 12966:         // The bound is `sizeof(void) + 6`, so the parse engine's answer is
 12967:         // readable as a count in the IR the way every other sizeof fold is.
 12968:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, folded_bound_type != 0 && folded_bound_type->kind == IR_TYPE_ARRAY && folded_bound_type->element_count == 7);
 12969:         // The IR engine's own answer, read off the type a `void *` points at:
 12970:         // this is the number the index the pointer arithmetic becomes is
 12971:         // scaled by, and it was zero.
 12972:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, void_pointer_type != 0 && void_pointer_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER);
 12973:         if (void_pointer_type && void_pointer_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER)
 12974:         {
 12975:             IrType* pointee = ir_type_from_id(&size_ir.program->types, void_pointer_type->element_type);
 12976:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, pointee != 0 && pointee->kind == IR_TYPE_VOID);
 12977:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, pointee != 0 && pointee->layout.resolved && pointee->layout.size == 1 && pointee->layout.alignment == 1);
 12978:         }
 12979:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, c_test_find_ir_function(module, S8("void_step")) != 0);
 12980:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, ir_validate_canonical_module(size_ir.program, module).error == IR_VALIDATION_NONE);
 12981:     }
 12982:     scratch_end(size_temporary);

 13526: // A sizeof operand is unevaluated, but every update still requires a
 13527: // modifiable real or pointer place. The same constraint must be reported by
 13528: // syntax-only and by either canonical-IR lowering form.
 13529: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_sizeof_update_operand_constraints(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
 13530: {
 13531:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
 13532:     struct
 13533:     {
 13534:         String8 expression;

 13536:     } cases[] = {

 13540:         {S8("sizeof ((int) ++42)"), false},
 13541:         {S8("sizeof (42++)"), false},

 13544:         {S8("sizeof (read_only++)"), false},
 13545:         {S8("sizeof (++ENUM_VALUE)"), false},

 13548:         {S8("sizeof (item ? ++42 : 0)"), false},

 13550:         {S8("sizeof no_link(++42)"), false},
 13551:         {S8("sizeof values[++42]"), false},
 13552:         {S8("sizeof ((const int){1}++)"), false},
 13553:         {S8("sizeof (++((const int [2]){0, 1})[0])"), false},
 13554:         {S8("sizeof (readonly_pair->member++)"), false},

 13556:         {S8("sizeof ((int) ++item)"), true},

 13569:         for (u32 form = 0; form < 2; form += 1)

 13571:             TemporalArena temporary = scratch_begin(0, 0);

 13574:                    " int probe(int *pointer, volatile unsigned char *narrow, int index) {{"

 17052: // GNU attributes written inside a type name -- the operand of sizeof or
 17053: // _Alignof, or a cast -- belong to that type name: they bind no identifiers,
 17054: // `vector_size` builds the vector both at compile time and in lowering, and
 17055: // the ignored `aligned`/`packed` keep the scalar layout, as clang answers.
 17056: // A `vector_size` argument binds block-scope names, and a typeof operand's
 17057: // attributes shape the operand's type exactly once.
 17058: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL String8 const c_test_type_name_attribute_source = S8_INITIALIZER(
 17059:     "typedef int Int4 __attribute__((vector_size(16)));\n"
 17060:     "enum { LANES = 16 };\n"
 17061:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))) == 16, \"vector size\");\n"
 17062:     "_Static_assert(_Alignof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))) == 16, \"vector alignment\");\n"
 17063:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(int __attribute__((aligned(16)))) == 4, \"aligned size\");\n"
 17064:     "_Static_assert(_Alignof(int __attribute__((aligned(16)))) == 4, \"aligned alignment\");\n"
 17065:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(int __attribute__((packed))) == 4, \"packed size\");\n"
 17066:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(short __attribute__((vector_size(LANES / 2)))) == 8, \"constant vector size\");\n"
 17067:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(typeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16))))) == 16, \"typeof vector size\");\n"
 17068:     "_Static_assert(_Alignof(typeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16))))) == 16, \"typeof vector alignment\");\n"
 17069:     "enum { VECTOR_BYTES = sizeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(32)))) };\n"
 17070:     "_Static_assert(VECTOR_BYTES == 32, \"enumerator vector size\");\n"
 17071:     "static long long pair[2] = {5, 6};\n"
 17072:     "int main(void)\n"
 17073:     "{\n"
 17074:     "    Int4 value = {1, 2, 3, 4};\n"
 17075:     "    Int4 sum = value + (int __attribute__((vector_size(16))))value;\n"
 17076:     "    Int4 bits = (int __attribute__((vector_size(LANES))))(*(long long __attribute__((vector_size(16))) *)pair);\n"
 17077:     "    int failures = 0;\n"
 17078:     "    failures |= sizeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))) != 16;\n"
 17079:     "    failures |= _Alignof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))) != 16;\n"
 17080:     "    failures |= sizeof(int __attribute__((aligned(16)))) != 4;\n"
 17081:     "    failures |= _Alignof(int __attribute__((aligned(16)))) != 4;\n"
 17082:     "    failures |= sizeof(int __attribute__((packed))) != 4;\n"
 17083:     "    failures |= sizeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16))) *) != sizeof(void *);\n"
 17084:     "    failures |= (int)(long __attribute__((aligned(16))))7 != 7;\n"
 17085:     "    failures |= sum[3] != 8 || bits[0] != 5 || bits[2] != 6;\n"
 17086:     "    int lane = 0;\n"
 17087:     "    _Static_assert(sizeof(typeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16))))) == 16, \"block typeof vector size\");\n"
 17088:     "    typedef typeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))) Lanes;\n"
 17089:     "    typeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))) cast = (typeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))))value;\n"
 17090:     "    failures |= sizeof(char __attribute__((vector_size(sizeof(lane) * 4)))) != 16;\n"
 17091:     "    failures |= _Alignof(char __attribute__((vector_size(sizeof(lane) * 4)))) != 16;\n"
 17092:     "    failures |= sizeof(typeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16))))) != 16;\n"
 17093:     "    failures |= _Alignof(typeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16))))) != 16;\n"
 17094:     "    failures |= sizeof(Lanes) != 16 || sizeof(cast) != 16 || cast[2] != 3;\n"
 17095:     "    return failures;\n"
 17096:     "}\n"
 17097: );
 17098: 
 17099: // Casts of attributed type-name operands in a translation unit small enough
 17100: // that the expression-task capacity, sized by its token count, cannot hold a
 17101: // separate reservation for each nested operand range.
 17102: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL String8 const c_test_type_name_attribute_cast_sources[] = {
 17103:     S8_INITIALIZER("int main(void) { return (int)sizeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))); }\n"),
 17104:     S8_INITIALIZER("int main(void) { return (int)sizeof(int __attribute__((aligned(16)))); }\n"),
 17105:     S8_INITIALIZER("int main(void) { return (int)_Alignof(int __attribute__((aligned(16)))); }\n"),
 17106:     S8_INITIALIZER("int main(void) { return (long)sizeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))); }\n"),
 17107:     S8_INITIALIZER("int main(void) { return (short)sizeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))); }\n"),
 17108:     S8_INITIALIZER("int main(void) { return 0 + (int)sizeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))); }\n"),
 17109:     S8_INITIALIZER("int f(void) { return (int)(long)(short)sizeof(int __attribute__((vector_size(16)))); }\n"),
 17110:     S8_INITIALIZER("int main(void) { return (int)sizeof(int[1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1]); }\n"),

 21101:     // A bit-field declarator has exactly one place to carry a list of its own,
 21102:     // after the width, and reading that list as part of the width -- #693 --
 21103:     // left the width unfoldable, so the aggregate never reached a layout at
 21104:     // all. The numbers below are what it reaches now: `b` crosses the storage
 21105:     // unit `a` opened, so the packed spelling takes the next bit while the
 21106:     // unpacked one starts a new unit two bytes further on, and `tail` is the
 21107:     // member that separates them. `leading_bits` writes the list on the first
 21108:     // declarator of the same list instead, where packing a field that already
 21109:     // sits on a boundary moves nothing.

 21167:     // A packed bit-field whose bits cross every storage unit that fits has no
 21168:     // single-unit access at all -- not even a narrower one: thirty bits at bit
 21169:     // three of a five-byte aggregate leave no position for a four-byte unit
 21170:     // and do not fit in a smaller one. The access is then the bytes the bits
 21171:     // occupy, which is what Clang spells `i40` and lowers to a sequence of
 21172:     // ordinary ones; `access_size` carries the span and
 21173:     // `ir_field_access_pieces` names the accesses. The widths that land here
 21174:     // are exactly the ones whose byte count is not a power of two.

 21184:         u8 access_size;
 21185:         u8 piece_count;

 21187:         {S8("struct __attribute__((packed)) straddling { int low : 3; int high : 30; };\n"
 21188:             "struct straddling straddling_object;\n"),
 21189:          S8("straddling_object"), 5, 0, 3, 1, 2, 5, 2},

 21336:     // C reserves the zero width for the *unnamed* bit-field, where it declares
 21337:     // no member at all and only moves the next one to its type's boundary; a

 21339:     // reference compilers. Accepted, it laid out a member that occupies no
 21340:     // bits and could still be assigned and read back. Each spelling is its own
 21341:     // lowering because one report is issued per aggregate: the literal width,

 21515: // `_Float16`: the type the LLVM 18 FP16 resource headers declare, and the
 21516: // only real floating type narrower than `float` this frontend has. The three
 21517: // groups below are the contract: the layout every supported target gives it,
 21518: // the binary16 encoding of its constants -- every expected byte string here
 21519: // was taken from clang 18 compiling the same source -- and the specifier
 21520: // combinations that are not a type at all.
 21521: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_float16_type(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
 21522: {
 21523:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
 21524:     BUSTER_UNUSED(arguments);
 21525:     // The layout does not vary with the data model: binary16 is two naturally
 21526:     // aligned bytes on LP64, LLP64 and Apple alike, and the complex and
 21527:     // vector spellings are built out of it. The `long double` of each of
 21528:     // these targets differs, which is what makes them worth asking.
 21529:     String8 layout_targets[] = {
 21530:         S8("x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu"),
 21531:         S8("aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu"),
 21532:         S8("x86_64-pc-windows-msvc"),
 21533:         S8("aarch64-apple-macos"),
 21534:     };
 21535:     String8 layout_source = S8("_Static_assert(sizeof(_Float16) == 2, \"size\");\n"
 21536:                                "_Static_assert(_Alignof(_Float16) == 2, \"alignment\");\n"
 21537:                                "_Static_assert(sizeof(_Float16 _Complex) == 4, \"complex size\");\n"
 21538:                                "_Static_assert(_Alignof(_Float16 _Complex) == 2, \"complex alignment\");\n"
 21539:                                "_Static_assert(sizeof(__SIZEOF_FLOAT16__ + 0) == sizeof(int), \"macro\");\n"
 21540:                                "_Static_assert(__SIZEOF_FLOAT16__ == 2, \"sizeof macro\");\n"
 21541:                                "_Static_assert(__FLT16_MANT_DIG__ == 11, \"mantissa digits\");\n"
 21542:                                "_Static_assert(sizeof(__FLT16_MAX__) == 2, \"suffixed limit\");\n"
 21543:                                "typedef _Float16 half32 __attribute__((__vector_size__(64), __aligned__(64)));\n"
 21544:                                "_Static_assert(sizeof(half32) == 64, \"vector size\");\n"
 21545:                                "_Static_assert(_Alignof(half32) == 64, \"vector alignment\");\n");
 21546:     for (u32 target_index = 0; target_index < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(layout_targets); target_index += 1)
 21547:     {
 21548:         TemporalArena temporary = scratch_begin(0, 0);
 21549:         TargetParseResult parsed = target_parse_triple(layout_targets[target_index]);
 21550:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, parsed.error == TARGET_PARSE_ERROR_NONE);
 21551:         if (parsed.error == TARGET_PARSE_ERROR_NONE)
 21552:         {
 21553:             TargetDataLayout layout = target_data_layout(parsed.target);
 21554:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, layout.float16_type.size == 2 && layout.float16_type.alignment == 2 && layout.float16_type.bit_width == 16);
 21555:             CPreprocessResult preprocess = {0};
 21556:             CParseResult parse = {0};
 21557:             CIRLowerResult lowered = c_test_lower_source(temporary.arena, layout_source, S8("float16-layout.c"), parsed.target, &preprocess, &parse);
 21558:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, preprocess.diagnostic_count == 0);
 21559:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, parse.diagnostic_count == 0);
 21560:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, lowered.diagnostic_count == 0);
 21561:         }
 21562:         scratch_end(temporary);
 21563:     }
 21564: 
 21565:     // The two reductions issue #631 records: the scalar parameter whose body
 21566:     // reported its own parameter undeclared, and the 32-lane vector the
 21567:     // resource header builds `__m512h` out of. Both are `static __inline__`,
 21568:     // so they are analyzed and then never emitted, which is exactly how the
 21569:     // header's intrinsic bodies arrive.
 21570:     String8 reductions[] = {
 21571:         S8("static __inline__ _Float16 f(_Float16 a) { return a; }\n"),
 21572:         S8("typedef _Float16 half32 __attribute__((__vector_size__(64), __aligned__(64)));\n"
 21573:            "static __inline__ _Float16 g(half32 a) { return a[0]; }\n"),
 21574:         S8("static __inline__ float h(_Float16 _Complex a) { return (float)__real__ a; }\n"),
 21575:     };

 21585:         scratch_end(temporary);
 21586:     }

 21588:     // Constants, in the encoding clang writes for the same spellings. The
 21589:     // interesting rows are the boundaries: the largest finite value, the
 21590:     // first magnitude that rounds to infinity, the smallest subnormal, the
 21591:     // exact half of it that ties to even and reaches zero, and a tie between
 21592:     // two normals that rounds up to the even significand.
 21593:     struct
 21594:     {
 21595:         String8 source;
 21596:         String8 bytes;
 21597:     } constants[] = {
 21598:         {S8("_Float16 values[] = {0.0, -0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.5, 1.5};"), S8("\x00\x00\x00\x80\x00\x3c\x00\xbc\x00\x38\x00\x3e")},

 21604:         // The C23 suffix: the literal has the type, so no conversion stands
 21605:         // between the spelling and these bytes.

 21720: // The exact-literal bignum defines only the limbs below `count`.  Every value
 21721: // here starts with a sentinel in every other limb: a helper that reads a limb
 21722: // it did not write folds the sentinel into its answer, and one that writes
 21723: // past the limbs its work occupies leaves a sentinel missing.
 21724: #define C_TEST_EXT80_STALE_LIMB UINT32_C(0xa5a5a5a5)
 21725: 
 21726: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL void c_test_ext80_big_fill(CIrExt80Big* value, u32 const* limbs, u32 count)
 21727: {
 21728:     for (u32 index = 0; index < C_IR_EXT80_BIG_LIMBS; index += 1)
 21729:     {
 21730:         value->limbs[index] = index < count ? limbs[index] : C_TEST_EXT80_STALE_LIMB;
 21731:     }
 21732:     value->count = count;
 21733: }
 21734: 
 21735: // Whether `value` holds exactly `limbs` below its count and the untouched
 21736: // sentinel from `stale_from` up.  Limbs in between are ones the helper was
 21737: // allowed to write while working.
 21738: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL bool c_test_ext80_big_is(CIrExt80Big const* value, u32 const* limbs, u32 count, u32 stale_from)
 21739: {
 21740:     bool result = value->count == count;
 21741:     for (u32 index = 0; result && index < count; index += 1)
 21742:     {
 21743:         result = value->limbs[index] == limbs[index];
 21744:     }
 21745:     for (u32 index = stale_from; result && index < C_IR_EXT80_BIG_LIMBS; index += 1)
 21746:     {
 21747:         result = value->limbs[index] == C_TEST_EXT80_STALE_LIMB;
 21748:     }
 21749:     return result;
 21750: }
 21751:

 23494: // A syntax pass retains declarations/assertions, not one token-sized body
 23495: // frame array per function. Guard the storage bound, independent of timing.
 23496: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_parser_body_frame_storage(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
 23497: {

 23871: // Supplied hashes exercise collisions deterministically without changing the
 23872: // production hash or adding a mutable override to parallel preprocessing.
 23873: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_source_metrics_path_identity(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
 23874: {
 23875:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
 23876:     {
 23877:         CSourceMetricsFileSet set = {0};
 23878:         String8 first_path = S8("include/a.h");
 23879:         String8 second_path = S8("include/b.h");
 23880:         char8 copied_path[] = "include/a.h";

 23882:         set.rows[first].translated_bytes = 101;
 23883:         set.rows[first].lex_count = 11;
 23884:         u32 second = c_test_source_metrics_file_row(arguments->arena, &set, second_path, 1);
 23885:         set.rows[second].translated_bytes = 23;
 23886:         set.rows[second].lex_count = 7;

 23888:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, second == 1);

 23891:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, set.rows[first].translated_bytes == 101);
 23892:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, set.rows[first].lex_count == 11);
 23893:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, set.rows[second].translated_bytes == 23);

 23916:     char8 paths[C_METRICS_COLLISION_PATH_COUNT][C_METRICS_COLLISION_PATH_LENGTH];
 23917:     for (u32 index = 0; index < C_METRICS_COLLISION_PATH_COUNT; index += 1)

 24631:     // A tag specifier names a type exactly the way a primitive word does, so
 24632:     // a set that spells both, or two tags, violates the same constraint the
 24633:     // sets above do. `int struct S v` was accepted with the aggregate's
 24634:     // layout under a spelling it never had, and `struct S int v` was
 24635:     // accepted with no definition at all. The prelude occupies three lines,
 24636:     // so every specifier below is spelled on line 4 and the declaration that
 24637:     // must survive the refusal on line 5.

 25511: // Buster deliberately selects signed __int128 for decimal magnitudes above
 25512: // INT64_MAX. Test that established extension, not Clang's different raw-literal
 25513: // policy, and retain explicit unsigned suffixes/casts as independent controls.
 25514: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL String8 const c_test_integer_literal_policy_source = S8_INITIALIZER(
 25515:     "typedef __int128 wide;\n"
 25516:     "#define LO ((wide)9223372036854775808ULL)\n"
 25517:     "#define HI ((wide)18446744073709551615ULL)\n"
 25518:     "enum { literal_index = (9223372036854775808 >> 63) };\n"
 25519:     "int literal_designated[2] = { [(18446744073709551615 >> 63)] = 9 };\n"
 25520:     "wide literal_scalar = 9223372036854775808;\n"
 25521:     "wide literal_array[3] = { 9223372036854775808, 18446744073709551615L, 18446744073709551615LL };\n"
 25522:     "wide literal_conditional = 1 ? 18446744073709551615 : 0;\n"
 25523:     "wide literal_return(void) { return 18446744073709551615; }\n"
 25524:     "wide literal_echo(wide x) { return x; }\n"
 25525:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(9223372036854775808) == 16, \"wide literal\");\n"
 25526:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(18446744073709551615LL) == 16, \"wide suffix\");\n"
 25527:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(+9223372036854775808) == 16, \"promotion\");\n"
 25528:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(1 ? 9223372036854775808 : 0U) == 16, \"conditional type\");\n"
 25529:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(18446744073709551615ULL) == 8, \"unsigned suffix\");\n"
 25530:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(0xffffffffffffffff) == 8, \"hex candidate\");\n"
 25531:     "_Static_assert(sizeof((unsigned long long)9223372036854775808) == 8, \"explicit cast\");\n"
 25532:     "_Static_assert(sizeof(2147483648L) == 8, \"LP64 and LLP64 candidate order\");\n"
 25533:     "int main(void)\n"
 25534:     "{\n"
 25535:     "    wide local = 9223372036854775808;\n"
 25536:     "    wide array[2] = { 9223372036854775808L, 18446744073709551615LL };\n"
 25537:     "    int result = local != LO || array[0] != LO || array[1] != HI;\n"
 25538:     "    result |= literal_scalar != LO || literal_array[0] != LO || literal_array[1] != HI || literal_array[2] != HI;\n"
 25539:     "    result |= literal_conditional != HI || literal_return() != HI || literal_echo(18446744073709551615) != HI;\n"
 25540:     "    result |= literal_index != 1 || literal_designated[1] != 9;\n"
 25541:     "    result |= sizeof(9223372036854775808) != 16 || sizeof(+9223372036854775808) != 16;\n"
 25542:     "    result |= sizeof(1 ? 9223372036854775808 : 0U) != 16;\n"
 25543:     "    result |= sizeof((unsigned long long)9223372036854775808) != 8;\n"
 25544:     "    result |= (1 ? -1 : 9223372036854775808) >= 0;\n"
 25545:     "    result |= (1 ? 9223372036854775808 : 0) != LO;\n"
 25546:     "    result |= 9223372036854775808L != LO || 18446744073709551615LL != HI;\n"
 25547:     "    result |= sizeof(1L) != sizeof(long) || sizeof(1UL) != sizeof(unsigned long);\n"
 25548:     "    result |= sizeof(2147483648L) != 8 || sizeof(0xffffffffffffffff) != 8;\n"
 25549:     "    return result;\n"
 25550:     "}\n"

 26096:             c_lower_to_ir(artifact_temporary.arena, S8("c-frontend-artifacts.c"), artifact_tokens, artifact_parse, target_native);
 26097:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, artifact_tokens.diagnostic_count == 0);
